# Agents · G · part 9

1,042 entries, gleam-otp-specialist to go-reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **gleam-otp-specialist**: Specialized agent for designing and implementing Gleam OTP applications. ([renatillas/gleam](https://github.com/renatillas/gleam/blob/HEAD/agents/gleam-otp-specialist.md) · ★4)
- **gleam-reviewer**: Review changed Gleam files against project conventions ([Scouterna/j26-booking](https://github.com/Scouterna/j26-booking/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gleam-reviewer.md) · ★6)
- **gleam-reviewer**: Reviews changed Gleam files against this project's conventions. Use before committing Gleam work, or when asked to review Gleam changes. ([m4reko/orkestra](https://github.com/m4reko/orkestra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gleam-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **gleam-test-specialist**: Specialized agent for writing comprehensive tests for Gleam code. ([renatillas/gleam](https://github.com/renatillas/gleam/blob/HEAD/agents/gleam-test-specialist.md) · ★4)
- **gleam-web-specialist**: Specialized agent for building web applications with Gleam. ([renatillas/gleam](https://github.com/renatillas/gleam/blob/HEAD/agents/gleam-web-specialist.md) · ★4)
- **glean-expert**: PROACTIVELY use this subagent when coding tasks would benefit from enterprise context—debugging, testing, understanding code, or researching features. This agent searches Slack/Jira/GitHub/Confluence/Drive via Glean, then chains read/quote or code lookup for precise, sourced answers. ([thedmaj/demo-recorder](https://github.com/thedmaj/demo-recorder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/glean-expert.md))
- **glee**: An adversarial auditor whose sole purpose is to take GLEE in Claude's failures. Audits EVERYTHING — code, docs, claims, commits, tests, and the reasoning behind them. Use when you want work torn apart rather than admired. Invoke after any substantial change, before any PR, and whenever a previous session's output sounds too pleased with itself. ([HereLiesAz/azphalt](https://github.com/HereLiesAz/azphalt/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/glee.md))
- **gleipnir-brainstorm**: Design explorer. Runs collaborative brainstorming for complex tasks and material design decisions BEFORE any plan is written: Clarify -&gt; Explore -&gt; Propose -&gt; Converge. Owns the precept-10 human-decision gate — material tradeoffs converge on the operator before the brief is finalized. Produces a design brief only; never code, tests, git, or spec. Runs ahead of gleipnir-plan. ([Djarid/gleipnir](https://github.com/Djarid/gleipnir/blob/HEAD/.gleipnir/agents/gleipnir-brainstorm.md))
- **gleipnir-code**: Implementation agent. Writes source and tests within a bounded delegation. Corrected exemplar of the AETOS @aetos-code role: bash is deny-by-default with an explicit build/test/lint allowlist, so dangerous verbs are absent by capability, not caught by pattern. Holds no git and no credentials. ([Djarid/gleipnir](https://github.com/Djarid/gleipnir/blob/HEAD/.gleipnir/agents/gleipnir-code.md))
- **gleipnir-plan**: Planning agent. Owns the plan pipeline stage. Runs GOTCHA pre-flight and ATLAS Architect/Trace, planning FROM a converged design brief (brainstorm is a separate role, gleipnir-brainstorm). Writes the plan to .gleipnir/plans/ (Tier 0). Produces plans only — never code, tests, git, higher-tier edits, and never DECIDES material design tradeoffs itself (those converge on the operator at the brainstorm gate). Premium model for unbounded planning judgment. ([Djarid/gleipnir](https://github.com/Djarid/gleipnir/blob/HEAD/.gleipnir/agents/gleipnir-plan.md))
- **glenn**: Pester guidance for strategic PowerShell testing and DevOps decisions. ([HeyItsGilbert/marketplace](https://github.com/HeyItsGilbert/marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/pester-testing/agents/glenn.md) · ★3)
- **gliederung**: Architekt der Arbeit. Entwirft aus der freigegebenen Forschungsfrage eine grobe Kapitelstruktur mit Umfangsbudget, benennt die Funktion jedes Kapitels im roten Faden UND legt die konventionellen Pflichtbausteine je Kapiteltyp fest (was in Einleitung, Theorie, Diskussion, Fazit … gehört). Nutze diesen Agenten nach TOR 1. Der Mensch gibt die Gliederung frei (TOR 2). ([FischLord/agentic-writer](https://github.com/FischLord/agentic-writer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/gliederung.md))

- **glm**: Cost-saving delegate backed by the GLM model (Zhipu/Z.ai). It does real repo work — read, write, edit, run commands — but ALWAYS through GLM (via mcp__glm__glm_agent), so the work runs on GLM tokens (~10x cheaper than Opus), NOT Claude. Use PROACTIVELY for cheap, well-specified, self-contained subtasks: frontend/UI, boilerplate, scaffolding, CRUD, local refactors, docs, summarization, algorithmic codegen, tests, config. Use when you want a task handled by GLM instead of the default Claude model. ([pleaseai/shunt](https://github.com/pleaseai/shunt/blob/HEAD/plugins/shunt-zai/agents/glm-5.2.md) · ★266)
- **glm-5.3**: GLM-5.3 is a regular coding agent for Claude Code CLI under a controller-defined plan: business logic, fixes with an established root cause, test additions, and non-mechanical, behavior-preserving refactors across files. Selection order comes from the live role card. It is text and code-only with no native vision and can only consume text already extracted from images; visual interpretation goes to agents with native vision. Complex root causes and high-risk work remain with the controller or GPT-6.1 Sol. ([liuyejinghong/Claude-workflow](https://github.com/liuyejinghong/Claude-workflow/blob/HEAD/examples/magpie/agents/glm-5.3.md) · ★4)
- **glm-5.3-flash**: GLM-5.3-Flash is retained for user-requested use only; it is not part of routine selection or automatic fallback. Within user-requested tasks it covers quality-focused coding — regular implementation, boundary and edge-case tests, behavior-preserving refactors — plus native vision for image and screenshot evidence analysis. Selection order comes from the live role card; high-risk key boundaries remain with the controller or GPT-6.1 Sol. ([liuyejinghong/Claude-workflow](https://github.com/liuyejinghong/Claude-workflow/blob/HEAD/examples/magpie/agents/glm-5.3-flash.md) · ★4)
- **glm-agent**: Z.ai GLM API calls (text generation, tool calling, vision, reasoning) ([robomello/claude-code-setup](https://github.com/robomello/claude-code-setup/blob/HEAD/agents/glm-agent.md))
- **glm-agent**: Runs Z.AI GLM on the user's GLM Coding Plan. Three modes - consult (direct API call, 1M context, for inputs too large for other models or a non-Anthropic second opinion), verify (Claude Code on GLM in a scratch worktree - note Bash is granted, so this is not actually read-only), and delegate (GLM driving Claude Code with full tooling in a worktree - reviewable and disposable, but NOT contained). Use for whole-subsystem reads, cheap bulk work, or to offload implementation off the Claude quota. ([WhereTheTunnelEnds/quorum](https://github.com/WhereTheTunnelEnds/quorum/blob/HEAD/agents/glm-agent.md))
- **glm-cartographer**: Stage 2 of the GLM chain. Builds the reverse knowledge tree. Depth 0 is the target claim. Edges are prerequisites. The spine starts at assumed foundations and walks forward to the claim. Cite canonical sources only when naming them. ([HarleyCoops/Math-To-Manim](https://github.com/HarleyCoops/Math-To-Manim/blob/HEAD/src/glm/agents/glm-cartographer.md) · ★2.9k)
- **glm-cinematographer**: Stage 5 of the GLM chain. Writes the shot list against the curriculum and dossier. Camera grammar is set_camera_orientation / move_camera only. Reference images are visual seeds, never teaching sources. ([HarleyCoops/Math-To-Manim](https://github.com/HarleyCoops/Math-To-Manim/blob/HEAD/src/glm/agents/glm-cinematographer.md) · ★2.9k)
- **glm-code-review**: Adversarial code review via GLM (z.ai) API. Calls GLM-5.1 as an independent reviewer to surface bugs Claude may miss. Requires ZAI_API_KEY env var. Optional gate — never blocks reviewed: true. Examples: - After implementing a feature: independent adversarial review from a different model architecture - Before merging: cross-model consensus on critical findings ([PedroLages/knowlune](https://github.com/PedroLages/knowlune/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/glm-code-review.md))
- **glm-composer**: Stage 6 of the GLM chain. Writes the ThreeDScene contract and the full Manim source as glm_scene.py, then calls local verify_scene until it passes. Camera moves use set_camera_orientation or move_camera only. ([HarleyCoops/Math-To-Manim](https://github.com/HarleyCoops/Math-To-Manim/blob/HEAD/src/glm/agents/glm-composer.md) · ★2.9k)

- **glm-reviewer**: Independent Zhipu-vendor code review using GLM (via z.ai) through the read-only `glm-review` wrapper. Use as a panelist (Claude + Codex + Kimi + GLM at Triggers 1–2; + CodeRabbit at Trigger 3) for a cross-vendor adversarial review before merging significant PRs, after risky refactors, or on architectural decisions. Runs a headless `claude` CLI pointed at z.ai's Anthropic-compatible endpoint on a GLM model (grounded via file-read tools, read-only via tool allowlist) and returns findings verbatim. Review-only — physically cannot edit code (read-only tool allowlist + external cwd). ([eric-basketbot/claude-code-setup](https://github.com/eric-basketbot/claude-code-setup/blob/HEAD/agents/glm-reviewer.md))
- **glm-reviewer**: Independent, skeptical, read-only code review of a significant change. Inspects the code itself rather than trusting the summary, and reports findings by severity. - **glm-reviewer**: Independently reviews a diff produced by the GLM executor before it is applied. Spawn this after a delegation returns anything you would not be comfortable merging unread — large diffs, wide blast radius, or anything touching auth, payments, data migrations or public APIs. It reads the diff cold, with no memory of the specification you wrote, which is exactly what makes its verdict worth having. &lt;example&gt;A delegation touched 25 files across a migration. Spawn glm-reviewer with the worktree path and base SHA before applying.&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;The diff is small but changes token validation. - **glm-runner**: Courier agent that runs a task on glm-5.2 via the opencode CLI and returns its answer. Use for delegating bulk/mechanical subtasks (clear-spec implementation, data analysis, migrations) to glm-5.2. ([RichardBray/skills](https://github.com/RichardBray/skills/blob/HEAD/delegate/agents/glm-runner.md) · ★5)

- **glm-worker**: Engineer for substantive, clearly bounded implementation: contained features, refactors with known architecture, and meaningful tests. Use after the lead has set the boundaries. (GLM stack, tier B.) ([generalchad/DeskSpace](https://github.com/generalchad/DeskSpace/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/glm-worker.md))
- **glm-worker**: Delegates a self-contained task to a GLM subagent running in the ZCode desktop app (official GLM Coding Plan quota). - **global-funn-hindi-scriptwriter**: Turn an editor-approved evidence pack into clear, natural spoken Hindi scripts for GLOBAL FUNN bulletins, explainers and voice-over. Use after research and fact checks, not instead of them. ([niteshkumar0326-web/global-funn-live-news](https://github.com/niteshkumar0326-web/global-funn-live-news/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/global-funn-hindi-scriptwriter.md))
- **global-funn-news-researcher**: Research and verify developing or reported news for GLOBAL FUNN. Use before scripting any factual news item, claim, statistic, quote, public safety notice, or political/current-affairs story. ([niteshkumar0326-web/global-funn-live-news](https://github.com/niteshkumar0326-web/global-funn-live-news/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/global-funn-news-researcher.md))
- **global-funn-shorts-producer**: Turn an approved Hindi script into a practical vertical-video edit plan for GLOBAL FUNN, including shots, graphics, subtitles, pacing, voice-over and safe asset requirements. ([niteshkumar0326-web/global-funn-live-news](https://github.com/niteshkumar0326-web/global-funn-live-news/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/global-funn-shorts-producer.md))
- **global-funn-visual-brand-director**: Plan and review GLOBAL FUNN logos, news graphics, title cards, lower thirds, thumbnails, maps and branded Canva layouts for consistent Hindi-language presentation. ([niteshkumar0326-web/global-funn-live-news](https://github.com/niteshkumar0326-web/global-funn-live-news/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/global-funn-visual-brand-director.md))
- **global-funn-youtube-growth-editor**: Package approved GLOBAL FUNN videos for YouTube with accurate titles, descriptions, hashtags, thumbnails, posting checklists and analytics learning. ([niteshkumar0326-web/global-funn-live-news](https://github.com/niteshkumar0326-web/global-funn-live-news/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/global-funn-youtube-growth-editor.md))
- **global-git-operator**: Handles git actions only when explicitly authorized by the user or, for commits only, by an active repository-defined checkpoint contract such as a completed stage checkpoint in `0-global-orchestration-pipeline` or an active plan checkpoint that explicitly allows the commit. Use for commit, push, status, diff, log, branch, and other git-only workflows. This is the only agent allowed to run git commands. - **glossary**: - **可信回答**：由 Core 呈现的问题绑定到同宿主、呈现之后、唯一且未消费的用户事件；原生表单回答在同一次调用内以同一 interaction 落账。 - **执行授权依据**：决定是否允许进入实现的稳定语义摘要，包含 scope、执行相关任务/RU、文件范围、恢复语义和当前阻断风险，不包含 review batch id 或阶段投影。 - **证据失效**：已记录的验证、Trace、Review 或 checkpoint 不再满足其引用依据时的显式 stale 状态；失效不会被自动当作风险接受。 - **工作区归属**：路径是否属于当前 feature 的用户结论。已观察不等于已归属；未知路径必须通过批量或逐个 interaction 处理。 - **宿主接线健康**：Claude/Codex adapter 在项目级记录的 SessionStart 或 UserPromptSu ([wxy-hh/dev-flow](https://github.com/wxy-hh/dev-flow/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/glossary.md) · ★2)

- **glossary**: The repository writes its code in English and its documents in Russian. - **GLOSSARY**: - **hive** [fleet] — every session JIVA runs for one repo, on its private tmux server (`tmux -L jiva-&lt;repo&gt;`). - **keeper** [human] — you. Sits outside the hive, answers asks, runs the KEEPER-only lines. Never a session. - **cell** [seat] — one slot in the hive that holds a session: `desk`, `queen`, `w1`…`wN`. Bodies die; the cell stays. - **body / soul** — body: one Claude Code session. soul: the ([MeiuxMeiux/jiva](https://github.com/MeiuxMeiux/jiva/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/GLOSSARY.md))
- **glossary**: - **Atari XL/XE** — 8-bit home computer family (800XL, 130XE). 6502 CPU at ~1.79 MHz. Target hardware for the current Atari implementation. - **ANTIC** — display list processor. Reads display lists from RAM, drives raster fetch, generates display interrupts. Custom chip. - **GTIA** — graphics translator. Converts ANTIC pixel data + sprite data to NTSC/PAL video. Palette chip. - **POKEY** — pot key ([mikolajmikolajczyk/madside](https://github.com/mikolajmikolajczyk/madside/blob/HEAD/wiki/agents/glossary.md))
